# Change-detector test inventory

> Audit snapshot: `457ac435e1216d3f7cac76d4e856b7aa020b5ea8` (222 test files, 13,534 pytest definitions reviewed; 849 candidates).

This file tracks tests under `libs/code` that meet the definition in [Change-Detector Tests Considered Harmful](https://testing.googleblog.com/2015/01/testing-on-toilet-change-detector-tests.html): they restate implementation information and can fail after a behavior-preserving refactor without independently verifying correctness.

## Rubric

A test is listed when its decisive assertion checks one of these implementation details:

- exact private collaborator calls, arguments, counts, or ordering when the interaction is not itself a contract;
- private state, helper selection, cache shape, object identity, or internal composition;
- source text, AST shape, class layout, inheritance, bindings, or import placement;
- a copied constant, registry, prompt fragment, or other implementation-owned data.

Tests are excluded when they verify user-visible output, public return values, persisted state, filesystem or process effects, security properties, or an external protocol whose call or payload is the behavior. The inventory is intentionally a review queue: before changing a test, confirm that its interaction is not a deliberate compatibility or safety contract.

## Status and maintenance

- **Status:** all entries are `open` until rewritten, deleted, or explicitly retained as a contract test.
- **Stable key:** use the path and qualified test name; line numbers are navigational and may drift.
- **Resolution:** remove an entry in the same change that rewrites/deletes its test, or add a short disposition explaining why it is a real contract.
- **Refresh:** repeat the audit for new or materially changed tests and update the snapshot commit and totals.

| [`TestDeferredActions.test_auth_change_retry_resolves_default_when_name_absent`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L26005) | Incidental interaction | It pins the exact argument passed to the private `_retry_startup_with_model` mock, so any refactor of how the default model name is resolved and forwarded breaks the test with no behavior change. | Assert the startup retry occurs with the resolved default model, not the exact private call signature. |
| [`TestStartMcpLogin.test_deferred_login_runs_target_worker`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L26438) | Incidental interaction | It asserts the private `_run_mcp_login_worker` mock was called once with `"provider"` after executing the deferred action, so restructuring the deferred login to another private entry point breaks the test while the queued login still runs for the right server. | Assert that draining the deferred action starts the login for the requested server (observable via the login flow), not the exact private worker call. |
| [`TestStartMcpLogin.test_starts_worker_immediately_when_ready`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L26451) | Incidental interaction | It pins the private `run_worker` kwargs `group == "mcp-login-provider"` and `exclusive is False`, so renaming that internal worker group breaks the test while the login still starts immediately and non-exclusively. | Assert the login worker starts (and other workers survive) without pinning the private worker group name. |
| [`TestToastAnchoring.test_short_terminal_keeps_toasts_onscreen`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L30605) | Copied implementation data | It imports the private `_MIN_TOAST_ROWS` and asserts `margin_bottom <= 12 - _MIN_TOAST_ROWS`, pinning the private constant in the margin computation, so changing the headroom constant with matching layout adjustments breaks the test even though toasts stay on screen. | Assert only the user-visible invariant (all toast regions stay at y >= 0 on a short terminal) and drop the private-constant arithmetic. |
| [`TestFatalErrorRedaction.test_show_locals_disabled_by_default`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L30654) | Incidental interaction | It patches `rich.traceback.Traceback` and asserts it was called once with kwarg `show_locals=False`, so an equivalent redaction that filters locals after construction or uses a different rich API breaks the test while the crash output still hides locals. | Capture the rendered crash output and assert no local variable values appear, instead of pinning the private Traceback constructor call. |
| [`TestFatalErrorRedaction.test_show_locals_enabled_when_debug_env_truthy`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L30666) | Incidental interaction | Same mechanism as the default case: it asserts the `Traceback` mock received `show_locals=True`, so a refactor that enables locals through a different call shape breaks the test without changing the debug-mode output. | Assert the rendered traceback contains locals when the debug env var is set, not the exact constructor kwarg. |
| [`TestFatalErrorRedaction.test_show_locals_disabled_for_falsy_strings`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L30678) | Incidental interaction | Parametrized over six falsy strings, it asserts the `Traceback` mock received `show_locals=False`, so a functionally equivalent change in how the flag reaches the renderer breaks every parameter without changing what is printed. | Keep the falsy-string parametrization but assert on rendered output (no locals leaked) rather than the constructor kwarg. |
| [`TestFatalErrorRedaction.test_falls_back_to_super_on_import_failure`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L30695) | Incidental interaction | It patches `textual.app.App._fatal_error` (a private Textual method) and asserts it was called once when `rich.traceback` is unimportable, so any equivalent fallback structure breaks the test while the crash still renders. | Assert that a crash still produces some traceback output when the rich import fails, not the exact private super() call. |
| [`TestPrewarmAwait.test_await_prewarm_imports_waits_for_worker`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L30802) | Incidental interaction | It asserts the mocked worker's private `wait()` was awaited once, so a refactor that awaits the worker through another attribute breaks the test while prewarm still completes before server start. | Assert the observable sequencing (server start does not proceed until prewarm finishes) rather than the exact `wait()` await. |
| [`TestPrewarmAwait.test_await_prewarm_imports_swallows_worker_failure`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L30813) | Incidental interaction | It pins the await of the mocked worker's private `wait()` on the failure path, so an equivalent failure-swallowing structure breaks the test while startup still proceeds. | Assert that startup proceeds after a failed prewarm, not the exact `wait()` await. |
| [`TestPrewarmAwait.test_await_prewarm_imports_swallows_worker_cancelled`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L30826) | Incidental interaction | It pins the await of the mocked worker's private `wait()` when the worker was cancelled, so an equivalent cancellation-handling structure breaks the test with no behavior change. | Assert that a cancelled prewarm worker does not block startup, not the exact `wait()` await. |
| [`TestHandleExternalSignal.test_interrupt_calls_action_interrupt`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L31424) | Incidental interaction | It patches `action_interrupt` and asserts the private `_handle_external_signal("interrupt")` calls it, so an equivalent inline interrupt implementation breaks the test with no change to external-signal behavior. | Drive a real (or mocked-worker) interrupt through the signal and assert the agent is interrupted, not the private method delegation. |
| [`TestHandleExternalSignal.test_force_clear_routes_to_command_with_force_bypass`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L31432) | Incidental interaction | It asserts the exact private call `_submit_input("/force-clear", "command", force_bypass=True)`, so any refactor that achieves the same bypass through another private path breaks the test without changing the force-clear behavior. | Assert the observable outcome (queued work is cleared immediately even while the agent is running) rather than the exact private call signature. |
| [`TestMCPLoginCommand.test_mcp_login_command_routes_to_start_mcp_login`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L33501) | Incidental interaction | It patches the private `_start_mcp_login` and asserts `/mcp login notion` calls it with the exact string, so inlining login dispatch into the command handler breaks the test while the command behaves identically. | Assert the observable outcome (the login flow starts for the named server) rather than the private delegation. |
| [`TestResumeThreadCwdSwitch.test_resume_prefetch_failure_restores_server_backed_cwd_switch`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L38944) | Incidental interaction | It asserts the mocked spinner's awaits equal exactly `[call("Loading thread"), call(None)]` and `_load_thread_history` was not awaited, so an extra or reworded spinner update (invisible in behavior) breaks the test while the rollback still works. | Assert the observable rollback (cwd, thread pointer restored) and drop the exact spinner await sequence. |
| [`TestPromptClipboard.test_ctrl_r_binding_is_priority`](tests/unit_tests/test_app.py#L44262) | Source or layout | It asserts the exact `ctrl+r` entry in the `BINDINGS` class attribute and `priority is True`, so an equivalent registration of the shortcut breaks the test without changing prompt-recall behavior. | Rely on the existing pilot-driven Ctrl+R tests in the same class and drop the BINDINGS-structure assertion. |

| [`test_classifier_model_cache_is_bounded`](tests/unit_tests/test_auto_mode.py#L5004) | Source or layout | It inspects the private `_classifier_model_cache` dict's length and keys to check LRU bounding, so an equivalent eviction implementation with a different private structure breaks it without changing observable caching behavior. | Cover boundedness through the observable factory-call count across many spec switches instead of reading the private cache. |
| [`test_classifier_cache_keeps_recently_used_spec`](tests/unit_tests/test_auto_mode.py#L5301) | Source or layout | It asserts on the private `_classifier_model_cache` key set to pin LRU-over-FIFO eviction, so swapping the cache implementation (e.g. OrderedDict to a TTL store) with identical observable reuse breaks it without behavior loss. | Assert recency behavior via factory invocations (a re-hit spec is not rebuilt while an older one is) rather than the private mapping. |
| [`test_pending_interrupt_scope_survives_completed_scope_eviction`](tests/unit_tests/test_auto_mode.py#L6338) | Source or layout | It drives the middleware by calling the private `_emit_event_once` nine times to pre-fill the private eviction ledger, so a refactor that renames the method or restructures scope tracking breaks it without changing the pinned resume behavior. | Seed the eviction pressure through public middleware flows (or a narrow test hook) instead of the private emitter. |
| [`test_abandoned_approval_scopes_stay_bounded`](tests/unit_tests/test_auto_mode.py#L6578) | Source or layout | It asserts exact sizes of the private `_pending_event_scopes` and `_emitted_events` ledgers against private constants, so restructuring that bookkeeping while keeping equivalent bounds breaks it without observable behavior change. | Assert the observable consequence (interrupts still fire and events are not duplicated) and drop the private-size equality checks. |
| [`test_resolved_scopes_evict_least_recently_used`](tests/unit_tests/test_auto_mode.py#L6697) | Source or layout | It reads the private `_emitted_events` mapping to pin eviction membership and the _MAX_EMITTED_EVENT_SCOPES bound, so an equivalent eviction mechanism with different private state breaks it while emitted-event behavior stays the same. | Drive scopes through the public event stream and assert which events are (not) re-emitted after eviction instead of inspecting the private ledger. |
| [`test_classifier_policy_section_order_and_references`](tests/unit_tests/test_auto_mode.py#L6756) | Source or layout | It asserts on the textual layout of the private _CLASSIFIER_POLICY constant (paragraph order via index() comparisons and absence of double spaces), so reordering or rewrapping the prose without changing its meaning breaks the test with no loss of classifier behavior coverage. | Assert the policy's semantic requirements behaviorally (classifier decisions on representative batches) and drop the layout pinning. |
